Use after solving something non-obvious, closing an experiment, or finishing a hard task worth not relearning. ([Capx-AI/casa](https://github.com/Capx-AI/casa/tree/HEAD/skills/casa-compound) · ★51)
- **casa-cos**: The Chief of Staff. At the start of a session it quietly reads the whole business, then tells you in plain English where you are and the single most important next move and why, and ASKS before doing anything. It plans and proposes; it does not run work, spend, publish, or fan out tasks until you say go. Use at the start of a session, when you ask what should I do or what is going on, or when you want a plain-language read on the company and one clear recommendation. ([Capx-AI/casa](https://github.com/Capx-AI/casa/tree/HEAD/skills/casa-cos) · ★51)
- **casa-de-gemas**: Change, extend, or remix Casa de Gemas — a single-file Canvas 2D + Web Audio watercolor garden game that moves through four seasons of hidden gems, narrated by Oli the owl. Use when adding a gem, a season, a scene object, a held tool, a line for Oli, weather, a sound, a day-cycle effect, touch behavior, or when customizing the message, name, colors or layout of index.html. ([deepujain/casadegema](https://github.com/deepujain/casadegema/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★3)

- **casa-department**: Focus a work session on one part of the company. Name a department (Engineering, Growth, Finance, Brand, Success, Sales, Legal, Data, Product, Operations, Strategy) and Casa drafts that function's ready work in parallel, checks it against the quality bar, and commits what passes. Use for a growth day, a finance day, or any session spent pushing a single function forward. You stay present and in control throughout. ([Capx-AI/casa](https://github.com/Capx-AI/casa/tree/HEAD/skills/casa-department) · ★51)
- **casa-design**: Build and refine product UI with production craft. Reads the brand spec from the company brain, detects the codebase design system, plans the visual approach, builds with quality principles (typography, hierarchy, spacing, accessibility, copy discipline), verifies visually, and records design decisions. Use when building a landing page, app UI, dashboard, onboarding screen, or any UI at Level 2 or above. ([Capx-AI/casa](https://github.com/Capx-AI/casa/tree/HEAD/skills/casa-design) · ★51)
- **casa-docs**: Look up CASA task documentation and source code. Auto-invoked when a user asks a CASA task/parameter question directly, or when the radio-interferometry skill hits a question its own reference files do not answer. ([skunkworks-ra/radio-analyst](https://github.com/skunkworks-ra/radio-analyst/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/casa-docs) · ★7)
- **casa-em-ordem**: Casa em ordem sem virar faxina infinita - calendário de contas fixas e vencimentos, manutenção mensal e sazonal (filtro, gás, caixa d'água, extintor do carro) e divisão de tarefas, gravados em arquivo e lembrados na semana certa. - **casa-experiment**: Frame and run a growth, pricing, or product experiment with discipline: a hypothesis, one primary metric, a guardrail, a ship plan, and a read. Use when starting a non-trivial task, investigating an issue, or preparing a change in a CASA Standard repository. - **casa-help**: The one-screen orientation. Shows where the company is in plain words, what to run now, every command grouped by what a founder wants to do, and your autonomy settings in one line. - **casa-ideate**: Generate many grounded options for a company move (a growth play, a pricing change, a positioning angle, a feature bet), critique all of them with explicit rejection reasons, and surface only the survivors. - **casa-map**: Show and approve the personalized company build map (levels, parallel tracks, and status). Use when the user asks to see the plan, the roadmap, the build map, or says casa map. ([Capx-AI/casa](https://github.com/Capx-AI/casa/tree/HEAD/skills/casa-map) · ★51)
- **casa-next**: The always-on advisor. Returns the single next best action for the company, chosen for THIS founder by weighing the engine's eligible candidates against the founder's pulse and real situation, and REASONS why it is the right move for their stated goal right now (not a generic why). - **casa-parallel**: Run a big task faster by splitting it into independent pieces and working them at the same time.
